By framing decision-making as a Lagrangian action minimization problem spanning this energy field, we enforce strict compliance with vehicle kinematics while executing collision avoidance. Extensive offline evaluations on both standard (nuScenes) and long-tail (CODA) benchmarks demonstrate that Lagrange establishes a promising framework for robust, interpretable, and kinematically feasible open-world autonomy.

</details>


### [104] [Leveraging systems' non-linearity to tackle the scarcity of data in the design of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20323)
*Giancarlo Santamato,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Massimiliano Solazzi,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度迁移学习的新型振动智能故障诊断方法，通过周期性多激励级程序和新的数据可视化与增强技术，在强数据稀缺条件下实现高效故障诊断，并在铁路受电弓结构上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度迁移学习虽能高效构建智能故障诊断系统，但仍依赖大量标注数据，而机器或结构故障场景下获取充足数据极具挑战性，因此需要解决强数据稀缺条件下的故障诊断问题。

Method: 采用周期性多激励级程序利用实际系统的固有非线性，生成适合预训练CNN分析的图像；提出新的数据可视化方法及其增强技术以应对数据稀缺；在铁路受电弓结构上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验验证结果有效支持了所提方法在强数据稀缺条件下的故障诊断能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据稀缺场景下的振动智能故障诊断提供了一种可行方案，通过非线性特征利用和数据增强，降低了对大量标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Deep Transfer Learning (DTL) allows for the efficient building of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems (IFDS). On the other hand, DTL methods still heavily rely on large amounts of labelled data. Obtaining such an amount of data can be challenging when dealing with machines or structures faults. This document proposes a novel approach to the design of vibration-based IFDS using DTL in condition of strong data scarcity. A periodic multi-excitation level procedure leveraging intrinsic non-linearities of real-world systems is used to produce images that can be conveniently analysed by pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) to diagnose faults. A new data visualization method and its augmentation technique are proposed in this paper to tackle the typical lack of data encountered during the design of IFDS. Experimental validation on a railway pantograph structure provides effective support for the proposed method.

</details>


### [105] [SoftSkill: Behavioral Compression for Contextual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20333)
*Xijia Tao,Yihua Teng,Xinyu Fu,Ziru Liu,Kecheng Chen,Yuzhi Zhao,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoftSkill提出了一种冻结骨干模型的方法，将自然语言技能转化为紧凑的连续上下文对象（soft skill），通过可训练的软增量进行优化，以少量虚拟token替代传统Markdown技能文件，在多个任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Markdown技能文件虽然可读、可移植，但需要冻结的语言模型在推理时重新解释长篇文本，消耗大量token且效率低下。本文希望探索能否将自然语言技能初始化为紧凑的连续上下文对象，让模型更高效地利用技能。

Method: 提出SoftSkill方法：冻结骨干模型，将技能表示为长度固定的连续上下文对象（soft skill），通过下一个token预测任务训练可学习的软增量（soft delta），在推理时作为潜在行为先验（latent behavioral prior）以prefix形式注入模型。

Result: 在单轮设置中，长度为32的SoftSkill prefix在Qwen3.5-4B上相比无技能提示提升：SearchQA +8.3分，LiveMath +42.1分，DocVQA +1.3分；相比SkillOpt提升：SearchQA +5.2分，LiveMath +12.5分，同时用少量虚拟token替代了成百上千的Markdown技能token。

Conclusion: 部分任务技能更适合作为紧凑的潜在控制信号（latent controls）而非需要推理时重新解释的Markdown文本；但在agentic执行这种长程过程行为的情境下，稀疏轨迹模仿虽有信号但尚不能稳健地压缩复杂行为。

Abstract: Agent skills are commonly deployed as natural-language Markdown files that encode answer policies, evidence-use habits, and task procedures. These files are readable and portable, but they are consumed indirectly: for each task instance, a frozen language model must translate a long textual artifact into generation-time behavior. This paper asks whether a natural-language skill can instead initialize a compact continuous context object, refined by a trainable soft delta while the base model remains frozen. We propose SoftSkill, a frozen-backbone method that tunes such soft skills with next-token prediction and deploys them as latent behavioral priors at inference time. In our main single-round setting, a length-32 SoftSkill prefix on Qwen3.5-4B improves over no-skill prompting by 8.3 points on SearchQA, 42.1 points on LiveMath, and 1.3 points on DocVQA. Relative to SkillOpt, SoftSkill improves accuracy by 5.2 points on SearchQA and 12.5 points on LiveMath, while replacing hundreds to thousands of Markdown skill tokens with a few virtual tokens. We further study agentic execution as a harder boundary case, where sparse trajectory imitation provides useful signal but does not yet robustly compress long-horizon procedural behavior. More broadly, the results suggest that some task skills are better treated not as additional Markdown to be reinterpreted at inference time, but as compact latent controls over how a frozen model enters the task.

</details>


### [106] [Automating SKILL.md Generation for Computer-Using Agents via Interaction Trajectory Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20363)
*Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了从交互数据中挖掘显式技能库能否提升下游计算机操作策略。通过三阶段流程（分割GUI轨迹、聚类候选技能、训练技能感知策略），发现挖掘出的技能聚类在源基准上可读性高（8个聚类中5个纯度≥0.95），但可读性不等于可迁移：GRPO仅将技能步准确率从18.5%提升至20.5%，对BrowseComp+几乎无改善，甚至在关键源域指标上不如简单频率先验。研究将方法定位为诊断性研究，指出现有边界检测器、无序片段表示和离线奖励模型不足以实现可靠的跨域策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否可以从交互数据中挖掘显式、可检查的技能库，并验证这些技能库能否实际提升下游计算机操作策略的性能，解决可读性与可迁移性之间的鸿沟问题。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：1) 分割GUI交互轨迹；2) 将片段聚类为候选技能；3) 利用聚类注释训练技能感知策略。通过GRPO训练策略，评估技能步准确率、BrowseComp+等指标。

Result: 5/8的聚类在InteraSkill Workflows标签上纯度≥0.95，但GRPO仅将IW技能步准确率从18.5%提升到20.5%，BrowseComp+几乎无变化，关键源域指标不如简单频率先验。

Conclusion: 轨迹挖掘可以暴露可检查的技能结构，但当前的边界检测器、无序片段表示和离线奖励模型不足以实现可靠的跨域策略改进，该方法应作为诊断性研究而非实用方案。

Abstract: Explicit skill libraries make computer-using agents easier to inspect, but it remains unclear whether such libraries can be mined from interaction data in a way that improves downstream policies. We study this question through a three-stage pipeline that segments GUI trajectories, clusters segments into candidate skills, and trains a skill-aware policy from the resulting annotations. The mined clusters are readable on the source benchmark: five of eight clusters have at least 0.95 purity against InteraSkill Workflows labels. However, readability does not imply transfer. GRPO improves IW skill-step accuracy only from 18.5\% to 20.5\%, leaves BrowseComp+ essentially unchanged, and underperforms trivial frequency priors on key source-domain metrics. We therefore present the method as a diagnostic study: trajectory mining can expose inspectable skill structure, but the current boundary detector, orderless segment representation, and offline reward model are insufficient for reliable cross-domain policy improvement.

</details>


### [107] [Rethinking Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20381)
*Qian Zhao,Kunlong Chen,Changxin Tian,Zhonghui Jiang,Haitao Zhang,Chaofan Yu,Peijie Jiang,Mingliang Gong,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现E2M1格式存在“收缩偏差”（系统性负舍入误差），该误差会随层数累积并被随机哈达玛变换放大，导致FP4训练不稳定；而均匀网格（E1M2/INT4）可避免此问题。基于此提出UFP4训练方案，在1.5B Dense、7.9B/124B MoE模型上取得优于E2M1基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前FP4硬件路径（如NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin、AMD MI350）以E2M1格式为中心，但该非均匀格式存在未被充分认识的根本性缺陷——收缩偏差，导致训练不稳定。本文旨在揭示该偏差的成因与传播机制，并提出更优的替代方案。

Method: 1) 理论分析E2M1格式的几何不对称性导致系统性负舍入误差（收缩偏差），证明该偏差在层间乘法性累积且被RHT放大；2) 提出UFP4方案：对所有三个训练GEMM应用RHT，仅对dY使用随机舍入，采用E1M2/INT4均匀网格。

Result: 在Dense 1.5B、MoE 7.9B和MoE 124B长程预训练中，UFP4相比强E2M1基线持续取得更低的BF16相对损失退化，标度律分析和消融实验进一步支持了结果。

Conclusion: E2M1格式的收缩偏差是FP4训练不稳定的根源，均匀4位网格（E1M2/INT4）是更优的选择。未来加速器应将E1M2/INT4作为与E2M1同等重要的训练原语加以支持。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [108] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个结合EfficientNet-B0和注意力模块(CBAM)的深度学习框架，用于精子形态分类，在SMIDS和HuSHem数据集上分别达到90.2%和93.9%的准确率，并通过Grad-CAM++可视化提供了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育是夫妻不孕的主要原因，常与精子形态异常相关。现有深度学习模型缺乏可解释性，限制了其在临床中的应用。

Method: 采用预训练EfficientNet-B0作为骨干网络，结合卷积块注意力模块(CBAM)聚焦精子头部关键区域，并使用Grad-CAM++可视化突出影响模型决策的特征。

Result: 在SMIDS数据集上准确率90.2%（宏F1=0.913），在HuSHem数据集上准确率93.9%（宏F1=0.948），优于SimpleCNN和标准EfficientNet-B0。

Conclusion: 该准确且透明的框架可作为生育诊所自动化精子分析的实用工具。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [109] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用55种上下文感知的时间特征（如热稳定性、温湿度同步达标率、峰值压力时长、恢复速度等）替代原始传感器平均值，并结合分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型（部分池化跨中心共享环境效应），将IVF妊娠率预测误差从3-5%降至1.27%，并在跨中心迁移中实现R²=0.86及64%误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IVF妊娠率建模通常仅使用患者层面变量，而高分辨率实验室环境数据未被充分利用，作者认为这是一个被错失的机会。

Method: 1) 工程化55种上下文感知时间特征（滚动热稳定性、温湿度同步达标率、峰值压力时长、压力后恢复速度）；2) 训练分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化在亚洲和北欧两个诊所间共享环境效应，同时保留各中心基线差异。

Result: 1) 在61周亚洲诊所数据上，预测误差降至1.27%（原始平均值3-5%）；2) 在北欧诊所留出数据上，R²=0.86，35-39岁年龄组误差较朴素基线降低64%。

Conclusion: 结构化的环境监测包含具有临床意义且可迁移的信号，跨中心迁移学习可行。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [110] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了上下文示范如何影响语言模型的危害性合规行为，发现良性示范和有害示范不可互换，且示范内容、顺序和训练方法共同决定了模型从示范中提取的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明上下文示范可以越狱语言模型，但不清楚模型如何解释不同类型的合规示范。本文旨在深入理解示范组成如何驱动危害性合规行为。

Method: 通过混合良性合规示范（无害请求+有帮助响应）与有害合规示范（有害请求+有帮助响应），测试三种关于示范组成如何驱动危害性合规的假设，并在四个模型上进行实验。

Result: (1) 良性和有害示范不可互换，良性示范根据模型不同可能减少或增加危害性合规；(2) 偏好优化是阻止良性示范增加危害性合规的关键训练阶段；(3) 示范顺序表现出强烈的近因偏差；(4) 不同模型在拒绝与上下文学习交互的方式上存在差异。

Conclusion: 基于示范的越狱不仅有效，而且其工作机制取决于示范内容、顺序和训练方法，这推动了对模型如何从合规示范中提取信息的深入理解。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [111] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi-LCB，将LiveCodeBench扩展至12种编程语言，用于评估LLM跨语言代码生成能力，发现Python过拟合、语言特定污染等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCB仅支持Python，无法评估LLM在真实软件工程中所需的多语言代码生成泛化能力。

Method: 将LCB数据集中的Python任务转换为其他11种编程语言的等价任务，保持原有的污染控制和评估协议，并自动追踪LCB未来更新。

Result: 对24个LLM（包括指令型和推理型）进行评估，发现了Python过拟合、语言特定污染以及多语言性能显著差异。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB成为多语言代码评估的新基准，直接解决了LCB的主要局限性，并揭示了当前LLM能力的重大缺陷。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [112] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEdit 提出一种面向冻结流匹配TTS系统的终身适应框架，通过文本嵌入空间的token级扰动学习发音纠正，并利用现代Hopfield网络作为内容可寻址情景记忆存储纠正内容，在312个多语言专有名词基准上将目标词音素错误率相对降低92.7%，且不影响通用语音质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配文本转语音系统在部署后无法修正专有名词的发音错误——除非重新训练模型。本文旨在设计一种无需更新模型权重的终身适应方案，让冻结的TTS系统能够持续学习并修正发音。

Method: FlowEdit 在文本嵌入空间优化token级扰动作为潜在条件编辑（而非权重更新），将纠正内容存储于现代Hopfield网络（内容可寻址情景记忆）。推理时通过带相似性门控的软注意力检索纠正，支持模糊形态匹配。

Result: 在涵盖18个语系的312个多语言专有名词基准上，FlowEdit 将目标词音素错误率相对零样本基线降低92.7%，通用语音质量保持不变，且更正过程在单GPU上约15秒完成。

Conclusion: FlowEdit 证明了无需重新训练即可对冻结的流匹配TTS系统进行高效的终身发音纠正，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [113] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP提出了一种基于单一世界干预程序（SWIP）的反事实语义，用于扩展DeepProbLog这类神经符号系统，通过神经物化将固定上下文的神经谓词转换为普通ProbLog选择，并在单一变换程序上通过加权模型计数（WMC）精确计算反事实，避免了DeepTwin方法的内生重复，实验表明在MPI3D上实现了2.14倍推理加速，并在SUMO HOV实验中展示了神经校准退化对估计量的影响及矫正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DeepProbLog的推理是关联性的，缺乏反事实推理所需的因果语义。现有方法（如DeepTwin）通过孪生网络实现反事实推理，但存在内生重复导致的计算开销问题。需要一种更高效的、具有精确反事实语义的神经符号推理方法。

Method: 1. 提出单一世界反事实语义（Single-World Counterfactual Semantics）；2. 通过神经物化（Neural Materialization）将固定上下文的神经谓词简化为普通ProbLog选择；3. 应用单一世界干预程序（SWIP）；4. 在单一变换程序上通过加权模型计数（WMC）计算反事实；5. 基于有限基化和唯一支持模型假设，证明方法相对于学习到的物化结构因果模型（FCM）是精确的。

Result: 在MPI3D数据集上，与DeepTwin方法对比，针对12,000个查询验证了变换的正确性，并实现了2.14倍的推理加速（避免了孪生网络的内生重复）。在SUMO HOV实验中，发现神经校准退化会偏置插件估计量，而正确设置范围的随机化策略AIPW估计器能消除总体均值和ATE估计量的大部分一阶偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP为神经符号系统提供了一种高效且精确的反事实推理框架，通过SWIP和WMC避免了孪生方法的计算冗余，同时揭示了神经校准退化在因果估计中的影响以及AIPW等去偏方法的有效性。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [114] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LedgerAgent，一种将任务状态显式维护在独立账本中并在每次决策前渲染到提示中的推理时方法，用于提升客服领域工具调用智能体的策略遵循和状态管理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准工具调用智能体将任务状态隐式嵌入提示中，导致两种常见失败模式：状态信息过时/丢失，以及语法正确的工具调用仍可能违反依赖于当前任务状态的领域策略。

Method: LedgerAgent在推理时维护一个独立账本记录观察到的任务状态，将状态渲染到提示中，并在执行改变环境的工具调用前使用账本检查状态相关的策略约束，从而阻止策略违规。

Result: 在四个客服领域及多种开放/封闭权重模型上，LedgerAgent相比标准基于提示的工具调用方法提升了平均pass^k指标，在更严格的多轮一致性评估中增益最大。

Conclusion: 显式的任务状态管理能有效提升工具调用智能体的策略遵循能力和状态追踪可靠性，尤其在需要多轮一致性的场景下优势显著。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [115] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将DAAM框架适配到语音扩散模型领域，通过跨注意力归因方法分析风格化文本到语音系统中自然语言如何影响声学输出。研究在CapSpeech-TTS上提取25层和24个ODE步骤的逐令牌热图，分析了3,600种(风格描述,文本转录)组合，揭示了风格标记对波形生成的影响机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格化文本到语音系统虽能用自然语言控制语音特征，但单个词语如何影响声学输出尚不清楚。理解这一机制对于诊断故障模式和提高可控性至关重要。

Method: 提出针对语音扩散模型的跨注意力归因方法，将DAAM框架首次适配到语音领域。在CapSpeech-TTS上提取25层和24个ODE步骤的逐令牌热图，分析120个风格描述条件生成30个文本转录共3,600种组合。

Result: (1) 风格标记的时间方差低于内容/功能标记，确认全局条件化特性；(2) 风格注意力与F0和能量相关；(3) 风格条件化在早期步骤和深层达到峰值；(4) 注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，与风格重要性峰值同时发生，表明网络在最关键的风格阶段具有最大选择性。

Conclusion: 这是首个研究自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中跨注意力的工作，为理解风格化TTS系统的内部机制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [116] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究混合专家模型（MoE）在分布偏移下的校准行为，发现硬路由模型中专家级校准足以保证整体模型校准，但软路由模型不足；为此提出对抗性重加权方法以改善校准-准确率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽表明校准可提升混合专家模型的集成准确率和校准性能，但校准在何种条件下对MoE有效尚不明确，尤其是在分布偏移场景下路由机制与专家级校准的交互机制缺乏理论理解。

Method: 1) 理论分析硬路由和软路由MoE在分布偏移下的校准特性；2) 提出对抗性重加权方法，通过惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合结果的校准误差来优化模型。

Result: 硬路由MoE在广泛分布偏移下专家校准足以保证整体校准，而软路由MoE则不然；提出的对抗性重加权方法在平均性能和数据困难子集上均改善了准确率-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 专家级校准对MoE在分布偏移下的可靠性具有关键作用，但路由方式（硬路由vs软路由）决定了其充分性；对抗性重加权是提升软路由MoE校准鲁棒性的有效手段。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的“计算可识别性”框架，区别于传统的理论可识别性（基于渐近性质、无限数据等理想化条件），通过有限的计算搜索过程寻找经验估计量，在给定误差容限、先验分布和搜索过程条件下，判定因果效应是否可识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的理论可识别性依赖于渐近性质和理想化数学条件（如无限数据），无法应对小样本、模糊图标准则、混合观测-干预数据等实际场景。作者希望提出一种更细粒度、更实用的可识别性框架，使因果效应识别问题能够在有限计算资源下得到实际可行的判定。

Method: 提出“计算可识别性”框架，将识别问题转化为一个有限计算搜索过程：在给定先验分布和误差容限条件下，通过搜索找到经验估计量；若能找到满足误差要求的估计量，则认为可识别。通过多个实验验证该框架在不同场景下的适用性，包括小样本、模糊因果图、混合观测-干预数据、反事实数据与估计量等。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能够在传统理论可识别性难以处理的场景中（如小样本、模糊图标准则、混合数据、反事实估计）提供切实可行的识别判定，支持细粒度的实际识别问题求解。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性框架为因果识别提供了一种更贴近实际应用的判定方式，弥补了传统理论可识别性在处理有限数据、计算约束等问题上的不足。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [118] [Learning universal approximations for partial differential equations with Physics-Informed Broad Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19754)
*Zhiwen Yu,Derong Yang,Liujian Zhang,Kaixiang Yang,Peilin Zhan,Jianmin Lv,Jane You,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息宽度学习系统(PIBLS)，一种无需反向传播的偏微分方程求解框架，通过直接最小二乘优化实现比传统PINN快1-3个数量级的速度和更高精度，并提供了通用逼近性质的数学证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器计算成本高昂（依赖网格），而PINN虽无网格但收敛慢且优化不稳定，需要一种兼顾效率与精度的新框架。

Method: 提出PIBLS框架，将PDE求解转化为直接最小二乘优化，无需反向传播；针对非线性PDE改进算法；给出通用逼近性质的严格数学证明。

Result: 在线性和非线性PDE实验中，PIBLS比传统PINN快1-3个数量级，同时求解精度显著更高。

Conclusion: PIBLS为科学机器学习提供了计算高效的范式，是实时仿真和设计优化任务的实用高速替代方案。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) play a central role in modeling complex physical, biological, and engineering systems. While traditional numerical solvers are robust, they often incur prohibitive computational costs due to mesh dependencies, whereas recent Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) offer a mesh-free alternative but frequently suffer from slow convergence and optimization instability. To bridge this gap, this article proposes the Physics-Informed Broad Learning System (PIBLS), a novel backpropagation-free framework that reformulates PDE solving as a direct least-squares optimization. We improved an algorithm within this framework to handle nonlinear PDEs efficiently and provide a rigorous mathematical proof establishing the universal approximation property of PIBLS for these equations. Experiments on linear and nonlinear PDEs demonstrate that PIBLS is one to three orders of magnitude faster than conventional PINNs while achieving significantly higher solution accuracy. This framework provides a computationally efficient paradigm for scientific machine learning, offering a practical, high-speed alternative for real-time simulation and design optimization tasks.

</details>


### [119] [Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20442)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Egor Bugaev,Andrei Ermakov,Denis Derkach,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段进化算法方法用于PINNs超参数优化，第一阶段低保真度筛选候选配置，第二阶段仅对最有希望的候选进行标准梯度优化训练，在三个PDE问题上取得了更低的均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs存在收敛不稳定、训练平台期、对架构和优化超参数高度敏感等问题，其物理信息损失函数的非凸多目标结构导致超参数搜索成为带噪声的黑箱优化问题，传统局部或基于梯度的策略容易陷入次优区域。

Method: 提出两阶段进化算法方法：第一阶段使用截断epoch的低保真度训练快速筛选候选超参数配置，将超参数选择视为黑箱外循环问题；第二阶段仅对最有希望的候选使用标准梯度优化器进行完整训练。

Result: 在Advection、Klein-Gordon和Helmholtz三个方程上，该方法在固定计算预算下始终优于标准训练，取得了显著更低的均方误差。

Conclusion: 进化算法基于种群的探索能力及其处理混合、不可微搜索空间的能力，为PINNs超参数发现提供了更鲁棒的机制，两阶段方法有效结合了探索与利用，提升了求解精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.

</details>


### [120] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)框架，通过将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的符号程序，结合演化搜索和梯度优化自动发现PDE的解析或近似解析解，在五个问题上产生可解释的表征，包括Allen-Cahn 2D几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家理解PDE解依赖数学结构而非数值表格，传统解析推导需要手工逐问题处理，数值模拟和神经网络都无法直接产出这些结构，因此需要一种能自动发现PDE解析/近似解析结构的框架。

Method: ASYS框架：智能体将PDE理论、问题约束和积累的搜索经验转化为可测试的微分符号程序；通过演化搜索优化数学形式，同时用梯度优化拟合连续参数；实现自动化的归纳偏置注入而非盲目符号回归。

Result: 在五个问题（有界动力学、有限时间爆破、自由边界聚焦）上，ASYS产生了可解释表征，包括Allen-Cahn 2D几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，后者此前无闭式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了表征PDE解的新范式，超越了手工解析解、网格数值解和神经网络近似，能够自动发现解析形式或构建指导进一步分析的解析近似。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [121] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard)框架，通过上下文路由器和不确定性门控温度机制，从领域不对齐的大模型中进行多教师知识蒸馏，训练轻量级时序预测模型，在气象学、碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个领域显著降低RMSE，支持边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型在物理科学中因分布偏移导致零样本性能差，且计算成本高无法部署于边缘计算传感器网络。核心挑战：如何从不对齐的大模型中提取潜在结构知识来训练轻量级专业预测器。

Method: 提出Guard框架：1) 上下文路由器(Contextual Router)——基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关教师模型；2) 不确定性门控温度(Uncertainty-Gated Temperature)——当教师置信度偏离领域现实时自动衰减蒸馏强度的"断路器"机制。将多教师蒸馏重构为实例级决策过程。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度、能源电网四个气候关键领域评估，相比固定权重多教师蒸馏基线显著降低RMSE；即使教师零样本精度次优，仍能从领域不对齐的教师中成功蒸馏知识；这些不对齐教师在28.5%的最难实例上优于全局最优大模型。

Conclusion: 领域不对齐的大模型仍可作为有效的纠正信号，Guard框架实现了适用于资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [122] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）方法，在边缘设备上使用4-bit量化小语言模型压缩用户提示词，减少传输到云端大语言模型的输入token数量。在248条提示词语料库上评估，平均每次蒸馏节省99.9个输入token，响应质量在15分量表上非劣效于原始路径，每调用净节能约70-270 uWh。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的预填充阶段日益成为云端能耗的主要来源。许多用户提示包含社交性"脚手架"语言（礼貌标记、道歉性开场白、重复、建立融洽关系的语言），这些语言对人类交流重要但对机器推理携带的低边际信息少，存在"社会-语义差距"。现有方法未充分利用这一差距进行节能。

Method: 提出SPSD边缘端流水线：使用4-bit量化的Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M)小语言模型在本地对用户提示进行情感保留语义蒸馏压缩，再传输至云端Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct大语言模型。安全关键领域通过规则门控保守路由至直通模式。

Result: 在248条提示词语料库上，平均每次蒸馏节省99.9个输入token，146次蒸馏调用均有正向节省。盲评LLM法官在121对比较中：43%平局、28%蒸馏胜、29%原始胜，非劣效于1分阈值。余弦相似度均值0.682，中位数0.712，54.1%超过0.70参考阈值。每调用净节能估计70-270 uWh。

Conclusion: 边缘端提示蒸馏可有效减少云端LLM的输入token成本，同时在实用的非劣效范围内保持响应质量，为降低LLM推理能耗提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [123] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对医学扩散模型Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA架构上进行了全面的内核级性能分析，发现训练瓶颈集中在cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM核函数，存在内存访问、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率不足等问题。基于此，提出的TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局优化在A100上实现了高达100倍的SM周期和动态指令减少，Tensor Core利用率提升至9.98倍，IPC提升7%，且不损失合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在高质量3D MRI合成中表现优异，但部署时因每样本需数百次U-Net评估及高度异构的内核行为，导致GPU资源需求巨大。现有研究缺乏对医学扩散模型在内核级别的深入性能分析，亟需系统性地识别性能瓶颈并探索架构感知的优化策略。

Method: 在Volta、Ampere和Hopper三代NVIDIA架构上，对Med-DDPM模型进行内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动和分析器优先级分数估计的全面性能剖析；随后评估TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种架构感知优化。

Result: 优化后SM周期减少高达100倍，动态指令减少100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45倍提升至9.98倍，IPC提高7%（A100），且合成质量无损。

Conclusion: 通过架构感知优化可显著缓解医学扩散模型的GPU资源瓶颈，为后续高效部署提供指导。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [124] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型（PGM）之间的深层联系：ILL学习的规则集可解释为对可解释抽象变量的边际约束，其提升操作对应满足这些约束的联合分布族，而基于Shannon熵的提升则等价于对数线性因子图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为信息格学习（ILL）这一符号-概率混合学习方法建立严格的概率图模型解释，阐明其与图模型、最大熵模型的关系，并为后续推理、可辨识性和混合学习提供理论基础。

Method: 通过将ILL中的划分（partition）视为确定性商变量（quotient variable），规则视为该变量的边际分布；将一般提升（general lifting）定义为满足所有边际约束的联合分布可行族，特殊提升（special lifting）采用L2均匀性准则（与最大熵相关）；Shannon熵提升则导出由学习到的抽象变量索引的对数线性因子图。

Result: ILL学习的规则集等价于一组关于可解释抽象变量的边际约束；ILL的格结构本身不是贝叶斯网络（其边编码抽象的细化/粗化关系而非条件依赖）；ILL应被视为针对商变量的可解释约束因子图的结构学习方法。

Conclusion: ILL提供了一种结构学习框架，用于学习可解释的、基于商变量的约束因子图，这为推理、可辨识性和混合符号-概率学习开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [125] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于双参数Weibull框架诊断Transformer权重分布，通过AdamW更新推导出平方权重范数的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力），在自训练Pythia-70M模型上验证了对齐力主导上升阶段（占88-94%），并提出了从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力的样条位移方法（准确率约92-94%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdamW训练过程中Weibull权重尺度参数λ(t)为何呈现增长、过冲然后松弛的动态行为，揭示其背后的力学机制。

Method: 1) 从AdamW更新推导出平方权重范数的主阶三力分解：对齐力（权重与自适应更新方向的相关性）、注入力（自适应步长幅度）、衰减力（解耦权重衰减）；2) 在自训练Pythia-70M模型上使用真实优化器矩进行验证；3) 提出样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力在上升阶段贡献88-94%的绝对力预算；对齐力与衰减力在饱和阶段趋于平衡；样条位移方法以约92-94%的准确率恢复对齐力，约为朴素两点基线的两倍；RMS-to-Weibull重建偏移约5-6%；λ(t)峰值随训练数据一致性变化。

Conclusion: 成功解释了λ(t)的完整动力学行为，揭示了数据依赖的权重尺度增长成分，为理解Transformer训练动态提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [126] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多元零膨胀高斯分布的分布估计算法（ZIG-EDA），解决了EDA在稀疏黑箱优化中的空白，无需手工设计稀疏算子即可联合优化稀疏模式与活跃参数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典EDA无法处理稀疏参数空间（大多数最优解系数恰好为零），而现有稀疏黑箱优化器不得不重新引入手工设计的稀疏算子、双层优化方案、零阈值等，这与EDA避免手工设计算子的核心理念相违背。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为EDA的采样律，通过潜高斯模型分别建模指示维度与取值维度，捕捉稀疏模式、活跃参数间相关性及二者交互；并设计实用的基于摊销逆推的估计器，确保潜参数可识别。

Result: 估计器能准确恢复潜相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA比稠密高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA收敛更快、最终回报更高，且仅使用少量活跃参数找到控制器。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA成功将EDA推广到稀疏黑箱优化，免除了手工设计稀疏算子的需求，实现了稀疏模式与活跃参数的无层次联合优化。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [127] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合方法，在自对弈强化学习中加入少量人工演示（仅30分钟）作为正则化目标，以解决纯自对弈训练产生的驾驶策略与人类行为不兼容的问题。该方法比纯模仿学习少用2500倍人工数据，仅需单张消费级GPU训练15小时，就能生成与人类轨迹协调的驾驶策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自对弈强化学习训练的驾驶策略虽然无需人类数据，但会产生与人类驾驶习惯不兼容的"异类"行为。现有通过奖励工程和域随机化缓解这一问题的方法脆弱且劳动密集。

Method: 将少量人类演示数据（30分钟）作为正则化目标，叠加在最小安全目标到达奖励之上，形成混合训练框架。

Result: 仅需30分钟人类数据（比纯模仿学习少2500倍），在单张消费级GPU上训练15小时即可完成，生成的策略能与未见过的（held-out）人类轨迹协调。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据（"调味料"）即可有效对齐自对弈策略与人类行为，大幅降低对大规模人工标注数据的依赖，实现高效且兼容的驾驶策略训练。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [128] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE是一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过基于低花费临床数据的证据分类和不确定性量化，自适应地决定是否需要引入MRI或PET等昂贵模态，在保持诊断精度的同时将成像使用率降低50-90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期诊断依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI和PET获取成本高且不易普及，导致全模态推理在真实临床场景中不实用。因此需要一种能够在保证诊断准确性的前提下，减少对昂贵成像模态依赖的方法。

Method: ProMUSE首先使用低花费临床数据进行基于Dirichlet主观逻辑模型的证据分类并量化不确定性；当不确定性超过学习阈值时，逐步引入MRI或PET特征，通过Dempster-Shafer理论融合模态间的信念和不确定性，获得校准的多模态预测。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD任务中，ProMUSE取得了与全模态基线相当或更优的准确率，同时将MRI/PET使用率减少50-90%，实现了显著的成本节约。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的AD筛查解决方案，适合真实世界的临床应用。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [129] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cAPM，一种连续AI辅助起搏标测方法，通过任务无关的替代神经网络、主动学习和持续学习策略，跨多个室速目标传递知识，将起搏标测定位至临床容忍度（5 mm）所需的起搏位点从13.7个（SOTA）降至4.5个，成功率81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助起搏标测方法需要为每个靶点重新训练模型，无法在同一个患者或多个患者的多个室速病灶之间传递知识，导致标测效率低、所需起搏位点多。

Method: cAPM包含三个核心组件：1) 任务无关的替代神经网络，学习起搏位点到12导联ECG形态的映射；2) 主动学习策略，选择信息量最大的起搏位点来优化替代模型；3) 持续学习策略，顺序学习多个目标的同时保留先前目标的知识（可配合数据回放）。

Result: 在包含不同生理条件和心室几何结构的虚拟测试平台上，cAPM（有/无数据回放）平均仅需4.5个起搏标测位点即可达到81%的5 mm精度定位概率，而SOTA主动学习方法需要13.7个位点且仅达到38%的概率。

Conclusion: cAPM通过持续知识积累显著减少了起搏标测所需的数据量，为后续向体内临床前和临床研究转化提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

</details>


### [130] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于二级结构感知的图神经网络SSProNet，通过引入二级结构分配和能量过滤的氢键拓扑来改进蛋白质表示学习，在多个基准测试中优于现有图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的蛋白质建模方法主要依赖序列邻接或几何邻近性，未能充分反映蛋白质折叠背后的原理，缺乏对二级结构元素（α-螺旋和β-折叠）及其稳定氢键相互作用的显式建模。

Method: 构建残基级节点表示并增强二级结构分配；基于能量过滤的氢键相互作用构建图边；设计二级结构感知的图神经网络进行蛋白质表示学习。

Result: 在多个常用蛋白质基准测试上取得了优于现有图方法的一致改进；所学图连接与已建立的结构基序对齐，增强了生物学可解释性。

Conclusion: 融入二级结构和能量过滤的氢键拓扑为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning. Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

</details>


### [131] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLARouter是一种在线路由算法，能从生产系统中稀疏的单边用户反馈中学习成本最优策略，在严格满足SLA约束的同时将运营成本降低高达2.2倍，无需逐基准调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用的推理成本快速增长，用户对高质量响应的期望与成本之间存在根本性冲突。现有成本感知路由方法依赖完整反馈信号、离线训练、大量逐工作负载调参，且大多缺乏SLA保证或推理时自适应性。

Method: 提出SLARouter在线路由算法，利用生产系统中稀疏、单边的用户反馈学习成本最优策略，提供成本最优性和严格SLA合规的理论保证。

Result: 在多种LLM基准上，SLARouter满足SLA约束且无需逐基准调参，相比现有基线将运营成本降低最高2.2倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter有效解决了LLM推理成本与SLA质量保证之间的平衡问题，通过在线学习和理论保障实现了成本最优与严格合规。

Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

</details>


### [132] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个物理信息驱动的框架，从全场位移和反作用力数据中自动发现各向异性屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数形式，通过凸神经网络表示屈服函数并嵌入可微分的弹塑性应力更新中进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别各向异性屈服函数面临三大挑战：屈服现象无法在全场力学测量中直接观测、方向性标定需要大量加载方向、选择合适的解析形式十分困难。现有方法依赖直接应力空间监督或预设参数形式，缺乏从原始位移-力数据中自动发现屈服函数的通用途径。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：(1) 用凸神经网络表示屈服函数，强制凸性和一次正齐次性，并施加拉压对称性假设；(2) 将神经网络嵌入可微分的弹塑性应力积分流程中，实现端到端可微训练；(3) 使用物理信息的力平衡损失函数（残差力）在多个加载工况下进行训练，无需屈服面直接数据。

Result: 通过von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d三种屈服函数的有限元基准验证，框架能准确识别屈服轮廓，对位移噪声具有鲁棒性，通过塑性活跃应力状态可辨识，具备不确定性量化能力，并可部署为多项式代理模型。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条从位移和力数据发现各向异性屈服函数的力学约束路径，将识别出的屈服函数保留在弹塑性应力积分结构内，为实验数据驱动的屈服建模提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

</details>


### [133] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx是一个仅140M参数的流匹配模型，通过轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接，在酶设计任务上以更低的训练成本（682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4）超越了Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3，并在成功率、结构新颖性、多样性和几何有效性上均取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全原子生成模型继承了结构预测中的昂贵架构，训练成本高且样本多样性有限。作者认为生成器仅需处理稀疏几何约束而非丰富的共进化信号，因此可以大幅简化模型结构。

Method: 提出Emyx，一个140M参数的条件流匹配模型。将模型能力集中在标准Transformer模块中，用轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接替代厚重的嵌入堆叠。此外，推导了流匹配插值到EDM噪声水平的精确重参数化，将流匹配的训练效率与扩散模型的最先进采样方法结合起来，无需重新训练。

Result: 在AME酶设计基准上，Emyx在成功率（需同时满足整体折叠恢复和催化几何精度）、结构新颖性、支架多样性和几何有效性方面均优于Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3。训练仅需682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4。

Conclusion: 生成模型无需继承结构预测的复杂架构，通过轻量级设计和精确的重参数化可以实现更高效、更优的酶设计性能。

Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining. Despite being the smallest model, Emyx outperforms both Proteína-Complexa and RFdiffusion3 against the AME enzyme design benchmark across success rate under strict evaluation requiring both global fold recovery and catalytic geometry accuracy, structural novelty, scaffold diversity, and geometric validity, while training in just $682$ GPU-hours, roughly $4\times$ less than RFdiffusion3.

</details>


### [134] [A Hybrid GNN-FEM Framework for Phase-Field Fracture Simulation. Physics-Preserving Hybridization for Generalizable Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19378)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Yongjin Choi,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合GNN-FEM框架，用图神经网络替代相场更新步骤，保留FEM位移求解器，实现了高效且可泛化的相场断裂模拟，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂方法虽能鲁棒模拟复杂裂纹演化，但求解耦合、非线性、历史依赖系统导致计算成本极高，限制了实际应用。

Method: 在传统交错格式中，用GNN替代每个载荷增量下的相场更新，保留FEM位移求解器以保证力学平衡；采用无量纲特征设计、基于网格的图表示和物理信息损失函数。

Result: 在多种几何、载荷、材料属性和离散化设置下均表现出强泛化能力，相比传统FEM降低了计算成本同时保持准确性。

Conclusion: 选择性替代策略（仅替代相场更新）比暴力数据生成学习完整断裂轨迹更有效，利用增量求解结构实现了历史依赖问题的物理一致性。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating simulations of complex physical systems, yet achieving physically consistent and generalizable predictions for nonlinear, history-dependent problems remains a central challenge. In this study, we propose a hybrid GNN--FEM framework for efficient and generalizable phase-field fracture modeling. While phase-field approaches provide a robust variational framework for simulating complex crack evolution, their high computational cost limits practical applications because they require solving coupled, nonlinear, and history-dependent systems within an incremental finite element procedure. To address this challenge, a graph neural network surrogate is integrated into the conventional staggered scheme, replacing the phase-field update at each load increment while retaining the FEM-based displacement solver to enforce mechanical equilibrium and boundary conditions. By preserving the incremental solution structure, the framework remains consistent with history-dependent fracture evolution without requiring the surrogate to approximate the full solution trajectory. This selective surrogate strategy emphasizes the identification of a physically meaningful and incrementally structured learning target, rather than relying on brute-force data generation to learn the full fracture process. The proposed framework achieves strong generalization across varying geometries, loading conditions, material properties, and discretizations through dimensionless feature design, a graph-based formulation on mesh-based domains, and a physics-informed loss derived from the governing phase-field equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the hybrid approach reduces computational cost while maintaining accuracy compared with conventional FEM, and exhibits robust predictive performance across diverse problem settings.

</details>


### [135] [How Linear Is a Transformer Feed-Forward Block? Per-Block Linear Recoverability Is Learned, Not Architectural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19379)
*Stuart Whipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性可恢复性(R²_lin)来衡量Transformer FFN层的非线性程度，发现不同层的非线性差异很大且与激活函数无关，并利用该指标指导模型压缩和揭示方法学陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer FFN常被视为非线性计算存储单元，但训练后的FFN块实际非线性程度鲜有定量测量。作者希望建立一个无需优化器的度量来评估FFN的线性程度，并探索其应用价值。

Method: 将每个FFN视为位置级的输入-输出映射，分解为精确的最小二乘线性近似加上残差。用闭式线性映射解释的留出方差定义块的线性可恢复性(R²_lin)。使用低秩双线性探针对残差进行进一步分析。

Result: GPT-2、Pythia-160m和llama-160m共12个块中，R²_lin高度异质且随深度非单调变化，范围从近线性(>0.99)到强非线性(<0.3)；相同宽度GELU模型具有不同轮廓，表明可恢复性为学习到的属性；低秩双线性探针仅恢复少量R²，与残差非线性无相关；可恢复块可进行大比例单层压缩(8倍参数减少)，低可恢复块则标记不安全区域；训练线性基线在病态激活上欠收敛。

Conclusion: R²_lin是一个有效的、无需优化器的FFN非线性度量指标，可用于指导模型压缩，并揭示了使用训练线性基线时的方法学陷阱。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward networks (FFNs) are often treated as nonlinear stores of computation, yet how nonlinear a trained FFN block actually is has rarely been measured. We treat each FFN as a position-wise input-to-output map and split it into the exact least-squares linear approximation plus a residual. The held-out variance the closed-form linear map explains defines a block's linear recoverability (R^2_lin), an optimiser-free measure of its linearity.
  Across all twelve blocks of GPT-2, Pythia-160m, and llama-160m, R^2_lin is highly heterogeneous and non-monotone with depth, ranging from near-linear (>0.99) to strongly nonlinear (<0.3) between adjacent blocks, and is not set by the activation function: same-width GELU models GPT-2 and Pythia-160m have sharply different profiles, so recoverability is a learned property of individual trained blocks, not an architectural one. A low-rank bilinear probe of the residual recovers only a few points of R^2, with gain uncorrelated with residual nonlinearity: the unrecovered computation is not a single position-wise product but higher-order or distributed structure.
  The measurement also serves as a targeted compression signal: recoverable blocks admit large single-layer replacements (GPT-2's early FFN at 8x fewer parameters for +0.77 perplexity), while low-recoverability blocks flag where this is unsafe. It further exposes a methodological pitfall: trained linear baselines can badly under-converge on ill-conditioned transformer activations, so we report the exact closed-form least-squares ceiling throughout.

</details>


### [136] [VERITAS: Verifier-Guided Proof Search for Zero-Shot Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19399)
*Manish Acharya,Zhenyu Liao,Yueke Zhang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERITAS是一个零样本框架，通过两阶段协议（Best-of-N采样+批评引导的MCTS）将验证器信号完整反馈到证明搜索中，在miniF2F上达到40.6%准确率，并提出了包含55个定理的组合学基准VERITAS-CombiBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的形式化证明器常常将丰富的验证器信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）简化为二元的通过/失败信号，导致大量有用信息被丢弃，限制了证明搜索的效率。

Method: 提出VERITAS框架，采用两阶段协议：第一阶段进行Best-of-N采样，第二阶段使用批评引导的MCTS，将第一阶段的失败案例作为显式负例输入，从而利用验证器反馈指导搜索方向。

Result: 在miniF2F上达到40.6%（Best-of-5为36.9%，Portfolio为26.2%）；在自创的55定理组合学基准VERITAS-CombiBench上达到7.3%（Best-of-5仅1.8%，Portfolio为3.6%）。

Conclusion: VERITAS通过两阶段协议有效利用了验证器反馈信号，在标准基准和需要迭代恢复引理名称的困难基准上均优于现有方法，证明反馈驱动的探索策略比无引导采样更有效。

Abstract: LLM-based formal provers often collapse rich verifier signals (syntax errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress) into a binary pass/fail bit. We present VERITAS, a zero-shot framework that routes every verifier signal back into proof search through a two-phase protocol: Best-of-N sampling first, then a critic-guided MCTS pass that ingests Phase 1 failures as explicit negative examples. The protocol preserves every theorem solved by its own Phase 1 sweep, so Phase 2's additional solves are attributable to feedback-driven exploration. VERITAS reaches 40.6% on miniF2F (vs. an independently run Best-of-5 at 36.9%, Portfolio 26.2%) and 7.3% on VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem combinatorics benchmark we release on which Best-of-5 (1.8%) falls below Portfolio (3.6%), exposing that unguided sampling hurts when correct lemma names must be recovered iteratively from verifier feedback. Artifacts are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [137] [Thermodynamic Signatures of Reasoning: Free-Energy and Spectral-Form-Factor Diagnostics for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19404)
*Salim Khazem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自由能签名（Fes），将注意力图谱拉普拉斯视为哈密顿量并提取热力学势与随机矩阵谱形因子，作为更丰富的谱描述符用于大语言模型幻觉检测。理论上证明了Lipschitz稳定性、表达性及PAC界；实验上在六个开源LLM和六个基准上，Fes轻量探针的平均AUROC超越现有方法（比LapEig高+6.5，比GoR-4高+2.4），且在无监督设定下RMT偏离得分达到0.71 AUROC。RMT分析表明正确生成呈现Wigner-Dyson统计，幻觉呈现Poisson统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力图谱拉普拉斯谱的幻觉检测方法仅使用少数特征值或手工标量，忽略了谱结构中的大量信息。作者希望设计一种更完整的谱描述符，充分利用图谱拉普拉斯的全部谱结构来提升幻觉检测性能。

Method: 将每层注意力拉普拉斯视为哈密顿量，提取热力学势（配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容）及随机矩阵理论（RMT）谱形因子，构成自由能签名（Fes）描述符。在理论层面证明了Lipschitz稳定性、表达性以及有限样本PAC界。实验采用轻量探针与无监督RMT偏离得分两种检测方式。

Result: 在六个开源LLM和六个基准上，Fes轻量探针平均AUROC超越LapEig +6.5点、超越GoR-4 +2.4点；无监督RMT偏离得分达到0.71 AUROC。RMT分析发现正确生成具有Wigner-Dyson统计特征，幻觉具有Poisson统计特征。

Conclusion: Fes作为更丰富的谱描述符能有效提升LLM幻觉检测性能，理论上具有稳定性和表达性保证，实践中无需修改底层LLM即可部署。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) is deployment-critical, and recent work shows that the spectrum of attention-derived graph Laplacians carries strong signal about reasoning quality. Prior spectral diagnostics, however, summarize the Laplacian spectrum by a handful of eigenvalues or hand-picked scalars, leaving most of its structure unused. We propose Free-Energy Signatures (Fes), a spectral descriptor that treats each layer's attention Laplacian as a Hamiltonian and extracts its thermodynamic potentials partition function, free energy, spectral entropy, heat capacity together with the random-matrix-theory (RMT) spectral form factor. We prove three results: (i)~Lipschitz stability of Fes under attention perturbation; (ii)~an expressiveness result showing that Fes enriches finite spectral summaries and approximates moment-derived spectral functionals under explicit regularity and grid-resolution assumptions; and (iii)~a finite-sample PAC bound on the AUROC of a training-free detector built from Fes. Empirically, across six open-weight LLMs and six benchmarks, a lightweight probe on Fes descriptors achieves the strongest aggregate AUROC among attention-spectral baselines, improving over LapEig by $+6.5$ AUROC points and over GoR-4 by $+2.4$ points on average, while requiring no update to the underlying LLM. In the fully unsupervised setting, an RMT-deviation score achieves mean AUROC $0.71$, providing a label-free but weaker detector. A complementary RMT analysis shows that correct generations exhibit more Wigner-Dyson like spectral statistics, whereas hallucinations exhibit more Poisson-like statistics. The anonymized code and config are provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [138] [FlexLAM: Resolving the Bottleneck Trade-off in Latent Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19408)
*Takanori Yoshimoto,Yang Hu,Naruya Kondo,Tatsuya Matsushima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexLAM用可变长度潜在动作（通过嵌套dropout训练）替代固定容量瓶颈，在相同token预算下匹配或超越固定容量LAM，支持推理时调整token预算而无需重新训练，并改善了Ego4D过渡重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAMs使用固定容量瓶颈，存在权衡：编码过紧丢失对齐线索，编码过松在标签稀少时需解决额外变化。目标是构建无需新架构或损失函数的可变长度潜在动作模型。

Method: 用嵌套dropout（nested dropout）训练可变长度潜在动作，生成前缀有效（prefix-valid）的编码，优先捕获紧凑过渡结构，仅在需要时添加细节，无需新架构或损失函数。

Result: 单一FlexLAM在标准稀缺标签监督和低回报单任务对齐压力测试下，匹配或超越每个评估token预算下单独训练的固定容量LAM；支持推理时token预算调整；改善Ego4D过渡重建。

Conclusion: 可变长度潜在动作是固定容量瓶颈的无架构、即插即用升级方案，可应用于潜在动作模型、潜在动作世界模型和视频预训练动作接口。

Abstract: Latent actions provide a compact interface between action-free video and downstream decision-making, yet existing Latent Action Models (LAMs) force every transition through a fixed-capacity bottleneck. We identify a bottleneck trade-off: overly tight codes can discard transition cues needed for action alignment, while overly loose codes preserve additional transition variation that must be resolved when alignment labels are scarce or narrowly distributed. FlexLAM replaces this fixed capacity with variable-length latent actions trained by nested dropout, yielding prefix-valid codes that capture compact transition structure first and add detail only when needed, without new architectures or losses. A single FlexLAM matches or surpasses separately trained fixed-capacity LAMs at every evaluated token budget under standard scarce-label supervision and under a low-return single-task alignment stress test, indicating that FlexLAM is not merely adjustable at inference time but learns a better latent-action interface at the same token budgets. The same model supports inference-time token-budget adjustment without retraining, and FlexLAM improves Ego4D transition reconstruction. These results suggest that variable-length latent actions are an architecture-free, drop-in upgrade to the fixed-capacity bottleneck in latent action models, latent-action world models, and video-pretrained action interfaces.

</details>


### [139] [Spectral DPPs via NEPv: A Scalable Continuous Relaxation of Determinantal MAP for Diversity-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19411)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将DPP-MAP（行列式点过程的最大后验子集选择）问题重新表述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，推导出带有特征向量依赖的非线性特征值问题（NEPv），并设计了一种自洽场（SCF）迭代求解算法，复杂度为O((ndk+nk²)t)，实现了近乎线性的缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从海量候选集中选择一个小型、多样、高质量的子集是现代机器学习中的关键原语（如数据筛选、核心集选择、主动学习、上下文学习示例选择等）。DPP提供了原则性的多样性度量，但其MAP目标是NP-hard的，且标准贪心/采样算法复杂度超线性，无法应用于百万到十亿级别的大规模数据场景。

Method: 将DPP-MAP离散优化松弛为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，推导其最优性条件为一类新型NEPv（特征向量依赖的非线性特征值问题），并设计基于谱间隙的SCF迭代算法，每次迭代仅需矩阵-向量乘积。

Result: 算法复杂度为O((ndk+nk²)t)，其中t为迭代次数（通常很小），实现了近线性缩放，可兼容低秩和特征图核，适合大规模ML应用。

Conclusion: 本文聚焦于松弛、求解器和缩放分析的理论贡献，为后续大规模实证研究奠定基础；SCF迭代具有基于谱间隙的局部收缩保证，提供了原则性的迭代求解框架。

Abstract: Selecting a small, diverse, high-quality subset from a massive pool of candidates is a recurring primitive in modern machine learning -- data curation and coreset selection for training and fine-tuning large models, active-learning batch acquisition, prompt and exemplar selection for in-context learning, retrieval diversification, and experimental design. Determinantal Point Processes (\DPP s) give a principled, well-calibrated notion of diversity for this task, but their \emph{MAP} objective -- pick a size-$k$ subset $S$ maximizing $\logdet(L_S)$ -- is NP-hard, and the standard greedy and sampling algorithms scale superlinearly in the ground-set size $n$.
